# Claim ledger: conditional L1 to L3 (blind concept audit, round 1)

One row for every prose line, question, graded key, option `why`, hint, feedback line, explanation, caption, summary line and model-answer line of lessons l1 to l17, `fluency-l7-turning-around`, `review-1..3`, `homework-1..3` (with the performance task `h-two-filters`) and `problems-1..3`. Every string was read in full; the claim column is cut at 110 characters; a question has its own row only where the screen has no prose. In the basis column, "L1 script" is `docs/reports/conditional-L1-concepts.py` (likewise L2, L3) and "check_wrongs" is `app/tools/numbers_lib.py` run on the level (157, 212 and 118 checks, all clear). Verdicts: correct, wrong, imprecise, misleading, unverified. A `correct` row names the source the line was read against and, for a key or a slip, the recomputation; every non-correct row carries its own basis and its finding number in `docs/reviews/audit-conditional-1-3.md`.

Sources: GS = Grimmett and Stirzaker, *Probability and Random Processes* (3rd ed.), sections 1.4, 1.5, 3.1. BH = Blitzstein and Hwang, *Introduction to Probability* (2nd ed.), chapter 2 and section 3.3. MFE = McNeil, Frey and Embrechts, *Quantitative Risk Management*, chapter 8. Hull OFOD = Hull, *Options, Futures and Other Derivatives*, chapters 24 and 25. Scripts: `docs/reports/conditional-L1-concepts.py`, `-L2-concepts.py`, `-L3-concepts.py` (exact enumeration with fractions, seeded Monte Carlo, both code solutions executed).

Rows: 3284. Verdicts: correct 3238, wrong 2, imprecise 43, misleading 1, unverified 0.
